 {"content": "**Repository Summary**\n- **Purpose**: Odoo 19 custom addons to manage a sports federation (clubs, teams, seasons, tournaments, rules, officiating, rosters, results, public site, reporting).\n- **Location**: All addons live under the `odoo/` folder as individual modules prefixed with `sports_federation_`.\n-- **Primary docs**: See [odoo/CONTEXT.md](odoo/CONTEXT.md#L1), [odoo/TECHNICAL_NOTE.md](odoo/TECHNICAL_NOTE.md#L1), and the Workflows in [odoo/_workflows/WORKFLOW_TOURNAMENT_LIFECYCLE.md](odoo/_workflows/WORKFLOW_TOURNAMENT_LIFECYCLE.md#L1).\n\n**Architecture & Patterns**\n- **Module split**: `sports_federation_base` (master data), `sports_federation_tournament` (tournaments, matches, participants), `sports_federation_format / sports_federation_scheduling` (service/wizards), plus domain modules (`people`, `rules`, `officiating`, `rosters`, `result_control`, `public_site`, `reporting`, `finance_bridge`, `venues`, `discipline`, `standings`, ...).\n- **Model naming**: Domain models use the `federation.` prefix (e.g. `federation.player`, `federation.match`).\n- **Common patterns**: `ir.sequence` for identifiers, `state` selection fields for workflows, wizards for batch operations (under `wizards/`), `controllers/` for portal/website endpoints, and `security/` for groups/record rules/ACLs.\n\n**Key Files & Where To Look**\n- **High-level context**: [odoo/CONTEXT.md](odoo/CONTEXT.md#L1)\n- **Technical architecture**: [odoo/TECHNICAL_NOTE.md](odoo/TECHNICAL_NOTE.md#L1)\n-- **Workflows (authoritative behavioural spec)**: [odoo/_workflows/WORKFLOW_TOURNAMENT_LIFECYCLE.md](odoo/_workflows/WORKFLOW_TOURNAMENT_LIFECYCLE.md#L1) and the other files inside `odoo/_workflows`.\n- **Module READMEs**: each module may have a `README.md` under `odoo/<module>/README.md` with implementation notes and expectations.\n-- **Install logs / historical notes**: `_logs/` contains installation and setup notes per module.\n\n**Development Guidelines (how to implement changes)**\n- **Add/modify a model**: create/modify `models/*.py`, export package in `models/__init__.py`, add access rules in `security/ir.model.access.csv`, add any initial `data/*.xml` and register them in `__manifest__.py` `data` or `demo` lists, add views in `views/*.xml`, update `menu_items.xml` as needed, and add tests under `tests/`.\n- **Add a wizard or service**: put transient models under `wizards/` and views under `views/` (manifest `data`), keep service logic in modules like `sports_federation_format / sports_federation_scheduling` (no persistent models if not required).\n- **Add a controller / portal flow**: use `controllers/` and add templates under `views/` or `templates/`; respect portal record rules (see `sports_federation_portal`).\n- **Security**: always add or update `security/ir.model.access.csv` and any `security/*.xml` groups/rules for new models or new endpoints.\n- **Manifest hygiene**: update `__manifest__.py` `depends`, `data`, and `version` fields when introducing DB changes or new data files.\n\n**Testing & Local Run**\n- **Where tests live**: `odoo/<module>/tests/` when present (some modules include unit/functional tests).\n- **Run module tests**: use the Odoo test runner for module-level tests (e.g. `odoo-bin -d <db> -i <module> --test-enable`) or your established local development workflow.\n- **Release sanity**: include at least one focused test for new business logic (especially schedule generation, result pipeline, and state transitions).\n\n**PR Checklist (required before merge)**\n- **Tests**: Add/adjust tests covering the new behaviour.\n- **Security**: Update `security/ir.model.access.csv` and add group/record-rule XML if necessary.\n- **Manifest**: Add new data files to `__manifest__.py` `data` list and ensure `depends` are correct.\n-- **Docs**: Update or add a short README in the module and, if behaviour changes, update the relevant `odoo/_workflows` file.\n- **Small, focused commits**: Prefer atomic commits (one logical change per commit) and descriptive commit messages.\n\n**How Copilot Should Help (recommended prompts & behaviour)**\n- **Primary rule**: Prefer conservative, minimal changes that keep module boundaries intact. Avoid touching unrelated modules unless absolutely required and explain why.\n- **When asked to implement a model change**: return a plan and a single patch that includes: the `models/<file>.py` change, `models/__init__.py` export, `security/ir.model.access.csv` entry, `views/*.xml` (if UI), `__manifest__.py` update, and a new test under `tests/` that exercises the main logic.\n- **When asked to add a wizard or generator**: create a transient model in `wizards/`, a wizard view, and a small deterministic example in tests that demonstrates expected output (e.g. round-robin schedule for 4 participants).\n- **When asked about business rules or workflows**: consult and reference the Workflow docs in `odoo/_workflows` and the `TECHNICAL_NOTE.md` to ensure behaviour matches the documented flows.\n\n**Example prompts you can give Copilot in this repo**\n- **Add a model**: \"Add a new persistent model `federation.referee` with fields `name`, `certification_level`, `active`; add ACLs, a basic tree/form view, and unit tests. Show me the patch with files changed.\"\n- **Create a wizard**: \"Create a `round_robin_wizard` that generates matches for a stage; include wizard model, view, manifest update, and a unit test verifying match count for 4 teams.\"\n- **Fix workflow behaviour**: \"Investigate why standings recompute includes contested results; propose a minimal fix and tests to prevent contested results from being counted.\"\n\n**Notes & Expectations**\n- **Authoritative sources**: the `_workflows` documents and `TECHNICAL_NOTE.md` are the source of truth for business behaviour—use them before changing workflows.\n- **Keep modules decoupled**: the architecture intentionally separates `base`, `tournament`, and the `competition_engine` service; prefer adding features in the appropriate module.\n- **Ask clarifying questions**: if a requested feature impacts tournament rules, scheduling, or publication flows, ask which workflow (`_workflows/*.md`) should govern the behaviour.\n\n**Documentation Maintenance (required)**\n\n- **Always update docs**: For any code, data model, view, workflow, or behaviour change, update the relevant documentation files in this repository as part of the same change set (or in a linked follow-up PR). At minimum, consider updating `odoo/TECHNICAL_NOTE.md`, `odoo/CONTEXT.md`, the module-level `README.md` under the affected `odoo/<module>/`, and any affected workflow files under `odoo/_workflows/`.\n- **Doc scope**: Document API/ORM changes, new models, new fields, migration notes (DB changes), public website impacts, and any user-facing behaviour changes. Include brief examples or CLI commands for running tests or sample data when useful.\n- **PR checklist enforcement**: Fail-fast — do not open a PR merging code that introduces behavioural or schema changes without documentation entries. If you cannot edit docs yourself (e.g., lacking domain clarity), leave a clear TODO in the code and notify repository maintainers.\n- **Why**: Keeping documentation current ensures maintainability, reduces onboarding friction, and prevents accidental regressions in workflows that rely on textual contracts (workflows, assumptions, and operational steps).\n\n", "content_length": 7327, "filesystem_mode": "0644", "git_mode": "100644", "git_state": "tracked", "path": ".github/copilot-instructions.md", "record_type": "file", "sha256": "7e616893749fb5ae32de33c88010309516272742120b3b28cec305c140409842", "size_bytes": 7331}

-{"content": "# Delivery Roadmap\n\nOwner: Federation Platform Team\nLast updated: 2026-09-02\nReview cadence: Every release\n\n## Purpose\n\nThis roadmap turns the stabilized competition architecture into an executable\nsequence of release, product, reliability, operations, and usability milestones.\nThe ownership chain remains unchanged:\n\n`registration → format → calendar → scheduling → schedule approval → match day`\n\nPublished schedules remain immutable, role separation remains enforced, and\nportal/public access continues through explicit ownership boundaries.\n\n## Release Baseline Qualification\n\nStatus: **Implemented, pending execution on the committed candidate**\n\n### Objective\n\nCreate one reproducible candidate commit for which static validation, fresh\ninstallation, focused release surfaces, performance qualification, browser\nfocus tests, and the full standard suite all pass.\n\n### Automation\n\nRun after committing all functional changes:\n\n```bash\nscripts/ci/run_release_baseline.sh\n```\n\nThe script executes:\n\n1. clean-workspace preflight;\n2. static and contract validation;\n3. fresh installation and standard tests;\n4. core qualification;\n5. portal qualification;\n6. public-site qualification;\n7. performance qualification;\n8. browser and release-focus qualification;\n9. final full standard suite;\n10. machine-readable evidence capture per lane.\n\nEvidence is written under `artifacts/release/baseline/` by default. Override this\nwith `RELEASE_BASELINE_EVIDENCE_DIR` when CI provides a dedicated artifact\ndirectory.\n\n### Exit criteria\n\n- One immutable candidate SHA is recorded in every evidence file.\n- Test failures and errors are zero.\n- Static, ownership, workflow, migration, publication, and security contracts pass.\n- Performance budgets pass.\n- Browser lifecycle, finance, and public-site tours pass.\n- Local and GitHub release-candidate validation agree on the same commit.\n- The worktree is clean before qualification starts.\n\n## Release Candidate and Migration Evidence\n\nStatus: **Implemented, pending rehearsal with an approved backup**\n\n### Objective\n\nProve deployment and recovery against a representative pre-refactor database and\nmatching filestore.\n\n### Automation\n\nRun the complete migration rehearsal from a clean candidate checkout:\n\n```bash\nscripts/ci/run_migration_rehearsal.sh \\\n  --backup-dir /path/to/approved-backup \\\n  --rollback-owner \"Release owner\" \\\n  --rollback-trigger \"Invariant comparison or acceptance validation fails\" \\\n  --yes\n```\n\nThe command restores the source backup, captures pre-upgrade invariants, upgrades\nthe restored database, captures post-upgrade invariants, runs role-separated\nacceptance, restores the same backup into an independent rollback database, and\ncompares all evidence. Evidence is written under\n`artifacts/release/migration/` by default.\n\n### Deliverables\n\n- production-like database and filestore restore;\n- pre-upgrade invariant snapshot;\n- reviewed module upgrade;\n- post-upgrade invariant comparison;\n- role-separated operator pilot;\n- backup checksum and restore evidence;\n- rollback drill with named owner and trigger;\n- archived RC logs and machine-readable evidence.\n\n### Exit criteria\n\nThe upgraded copy preserves competition, registration, fixture, schedule,\npublication, match-day, result, standings, portal, public, integration, and\nattachment invariants. Restore and rollback complete from the recorded backup.\n\n## Simplified Competition Journey\n\nStatus: **Implemented**\n\n### Objective\n\nAllow a normal league, tournament day, or cup to be configured, scheduled,\napproved, and published without exposing the underlying Odoo model structure.\n\n### Deliverables\n\n- template-led competition creation;\n- guided Basics, Participants, Format, Calendar, Schedule, Publish journey;\n- user-facing Draft, Ready, Live, Finished presentation states;\n- visible blockers, responsible role, and next action;\n- advanced stage graph, revision, fairness, and shared-day controls separated\n  from the normal path;\n- task-first club portal.\n\n### Reversibility contract\n\n- Setup templates apply editable defaults and can be cancelled.\n- Late fixtures can be added to or removed from calendar allocations.\n- Draft and change-requested schedules refresh from the current calendar plan without deleting assignments.\n- Submitting planners can withdraw pending reviews; approver-requested changes return the same schedule to an editable state.\n- Published evidence stays immutable and corrections use a linked replacement revision.\n\n### Exit criteria\n\nA trained non-developer completes the normal journey without developer mode, direct model navigation, manual progression mapping, or shell access. Every pre-publication handoff has an explicit return path; post-publication changes use traceable replacement revisions.\n\n## Operational Job Reliability\n\nStatus: **Implemented**\n\n### Objective\n\nGeneralize the proven standings recompute reliability pattern without moving\ndomain execution out of its owning addon.\n\n### Deliverables\n\n- bounded retry and backoff;\n- stale-running recovery;\n- dead-letter/operator-action state;\n- correlation IDs and sanitized error evidence;\n- idempotent manual retry;\n- operator visibility for notification, report, integration, retention, and\n  snapshot jobs;\n- shared infrastructure only after two proven consumers exist.\n\n### Implementation\n\nThe shared `federation.operation.job` queue records source ownership, attempts,\nretry timing, correlation IDs, stale-worker recovery, terminal operator action,\nand manual retry. Notification and scheduled-report failures can be queued for\nautomatic retry. Failed inbound deliveries create operator-action jobs because\npayload and governance corrections require explicit review before replay.\n\nThe **Operational Job Health** administration view groups work by state and\nsource, exposes sanitized failure evidence, opens the source record, and permits\nexplicit retry only for failed jobs.\n\n### Exit criteria\n\nOperators can identify what failed, whether it will retry, when it will retry,\nhow many attempts remain, which correlation ID applies, and whether manual retry\nis safe.\n\n## Retention Evidence and Recovery Visibility\n\nStatus: **Partially implemented**\n\n### Objective\n\nTurn retention execution into auditable operational evidence and surface\nrecovery readiness in one place.\n\n### Deliverables\n\n- candidate, deleted, skipped, attachment, and failure counts;\n- duration, policy window/version, dry-run flag, and correlation ID;\n- read-only retention evidence views;\n- overdue and repeated-failure alerts;\n- release-readiness checks for disabled or stale retention jobs;\n- links to restore and rollback evidence.\n\n### Exit criteria\n\nThe release owner can prove that each policy ran, used the intended window,\nretained unresolved workflow evidence, cleaned matching attachments, and exposed\nfailures to operators.\n\n## Consolidated Operational Dashboard\n\nStatus: **Planned**\n\n### Objective\n\nAggregate existing release, job, retention, integration, and competition queues\nwithout duplicating their source records or commands.\n\n### Deliverables\n\n- release readiness and last evidence status;\n- queued, retrying, dead-letter, and stale job health;\n- retention freshness and outcomes;\n- failed or partial integration deliveries;\n- registration blockers, review backlog, unpublished approved schedules,\n  match-day publication gaps, result backlog, and standings failures;\n- direct links to owning records.\n\n### Exit criteria\n\nAn operator can assess release and live operational health from one dashboard and\nnavigate directly to the owning workflow for remediation.\n\n## Accessibility and Operator Usability\n\nStatus: **Planned**\n\n### Objective\n\nQualify the guided journey and principal portal/public workflows for keyboard,\nmobile, terminology, and accessible error recovery.\n\n### Deliverables\n\n- keyboard-only competition setup acceptance;\n- modal focus and focus-restoration checks;\n- linked validation summaries;\n- non-color status cues and accessible icon labels;\n- mobile portal qualification;\n- raw internal-state prevention;\n- deterministic training sandbox and operator walkthrough.\n\n### Exit criteria\n\nThe release pilot is completable by keyboard, principal mobile portal workflows\nremain usable, and user-facing terminology consistently describes the simplified\njourney rather than internal model states.\n\n## Change Control\n\nEach milestone must ship as focused, test-backed changes with corresponding\nworkflow, module, technical, and runbook documentation. New abstractions require\nmultiple proven consumers. Security checks, ownership boundaries, publication\nimmutability, and release validation must not be weakened to accommodate\nimplementation drift.\n", "content_length": 8677, "filesystem_mode": "0644", "git_mode": "100644", "git_state": "tracked", "path": "docs/DELIVERY_ROADMAP.md", "record_type": "file", "sha256": "fd9b848c61b3547d165a31196c8dfb4bacdf5a087f765b5a4e13ba3a89d88743", "size_bytes": 8687}

 {"content": "# Deterministic Release Pilot Scenario\n\nOwner: Federation Platform Team\nReview cadence: Every release candidate\n\nThis scenario is the P3 operational acceptance path. It complements module tests\nby proving that one federation storyline can be followed across ownership\nboundaries without hidden setup or direct database edits.\n\n## Automated gate\n\nRun the complete release-focus verification:\n\n```bash\npython3 ci/check_release_focus_contract.py\nscripts/ci/run_rc_validation.sh focus\n```\n\nThe focus lane runs the lifecycle, finance, and public browser tours together\nwith the schedule-amendment, approval immutability, officiating, governance,\nportal-security, compliance, and pilot-readiness tests.\n\n## Pilot storyline\n\n1. Create a season and competition edition.\n2. Open registration and finalize the participant set.\n3. Freeze the competition structure and generate fixtures.\n4. Prepare match-day capacity and assign every fixture to a slot.\n5. Submit the schedule for independent review and publish the approved revision.\n6. Assign named officials or a club officiating duty and resolve shortages.\n7. Open match-day control, record results, and approve official results.\n8. Recompute and publish standings.\n9. Confirm the public competition, schedule, format, and standings pages.\n10. Confirm finance-event creation, settlement, export readiness, and traceability.\n11. Amend one published schedule through a replacement revision and verify that\n    the original publication and audit evidence remain immutable.\n12. Verify that an unrelated portal user cannot read or mutate another club's\n    registration, roster, officiating, result, or compliance records.\n\n## Acceptance evidence\n\nRecord the release-candidate commit, database name, commands, start/end time,\nall automated results, and any manual observations in the release runbook. A\nrelease candidate is not accepted while any focus-lane test is skipped, flaky,\nor dependent on manual data repair.\n\n## Role-separated acceptance gate\n\nRun the operator-role contract before the browser pilot:\n\n```bash\nscripts/ci/run_rc_validation.sh focus\n```\n\nThe pilot must use distinct registration, format, calendar, schedule-planning, schedule-approval, and match-day operators. Planning and approval must not be collapsed into the administrator account. The browser tour remains the navigation smoke test; owning addon tests remain authoritative for state transitions and negative workflow decisions.\n", "content_length": 2446, "filesystem_mode": "0644", "git_mode": "100644", "git_state": "tracked", "path": "docs/RELEASE_PILOT_SCENARIO.md", "record_type": "file", "sha256": "480e87923b00194fbdefb81db0fd76d963668e73ca0a732514011408421d6092", "size_bytes": 2446}
 {"content": "# Competition Workflow Ownership\n\nThe ownership chain is the supported competition-planning architecture. No\nproduction data migration is required solely for this ownership clarification.\n\n## Capability ownership\n\n- Competition Core owns edition lifecycle and role assignments.\n- Registration owns entries and versioned finalized participant sets.\n- Format Studio owns stage topology, logical fixtures, standings snapshots, and progression.\n- Calendar owns physical match days, courts, and availability slots.\n- Scheduling owns fixture-to-slot assignments and fairness proposals.\n- Schedule Approval owns independent review and immutable publications.\n- Result Control owns the complete result lifecycle.\n- Match-Day Operations owns live execution and incidents.\n\n## Domain event contract\n\nEvents are past-tense facts. Producers emit only after a successful state change. Consumers must be idempotent and may not mutate the producer aggregate. Event payloads contain stable record IDs, version/revision, actor, and reason where applicable.\n\nRequired events: participant_set_finalized, structure_frozen, stage_standings_frozen, stage_progression_applied, matchday_capacity_ready, schedule_submitted, schedule_approved, schedule_published, matchday_opened, result_approved, result_contested, result_corrected, matchday_closed.\n", "content_length": 1325, "filesystem_mode": "0644", "git_mode": "100644", "git_state": "tracked", "path": "docs/competition_engine_ownership.md", "record_type": "file", "sha256": "3352ecae7e79ab9a7fa5079eca2e62c212b65a59136effde4e6945b21563894c", "size_bytes": 1325}

 {"content": "\"\"\"Loader for the governed HTTP-route contract.\n\nThe route contract is deliberately stored as JSON instead of being extracted\nfrom ROUTE_INVENTORY.md.  {"content": "\"\"\"Tour T-05: Season Registration — Club Self-Service to Confirmation\n\nWalks the complete season registration lifecycle:\n  1. Create a season and open it\n  2. Register a team (draft registration)\n  3. Admin confirms the registration\n  4. Cancel and restore to draft\n  5. Re-confirm (idempotent close of season also exercised)\n\nKey invariants verified:\n- Season starts in 'draft', transitions to 'open' via action_open()\n- Registration state: draft → confirmed → cancelled → draft → confirmed\n- Duplicate team+season registration is rejected by DB constraint\n- Season can be closed from 'open'\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom odoo.tests.common import TransactionCase\nfrom odoo.tools.misc import mute_logger\n\n\nclass TestTourSeasonRegistration(TransactionCase):\n    \"\"\"T-05: Season registration lifecycle tour.\"\"\"\n\n    @classmethod\n    def setUpClass(cls):\n        super().setUpClass()\n\n        cls.season = cls.env[\"federation.season\"].create(\n            {\n                \"name\": \"Registration Tour Season\",\n                \"code\": \"REG26\",\n                \"date_start\": \"2026-01-01\",\n                \"date_end\": \"2026-12-31\",\n            }\n        )\n        cls.club = cls.env[\"federation.club\"].create(\n            {\"name\": \"Registration Tour Club\", \"code\": \"REGC\"}\n        )\n        cls.team = cls.env[\"federation.team\"].create(\n            {\"name\": \"Registration Tour Team\", \"club_id\": cls.club.id, \"code\": \"REGT\"}\n        )\n        cls.club2 = cls.env[\"federation.club\"].create(\n            {\"name\": \"Second Tour Club\", \"code\": \"SEC2\"}\n        )\n        cls.team2 = cls.env[\"federation.team\"].create(\n            {\"name\": \"Second Tour Team\", \"club_id\": cls.club2.id, \"code\": \"SETT2\"}\n        )\n\n    def test_season_registration_full_lifecycle(self):\n        \"\"\"Season registration: open season → register → confirm → cancel → re-confirm.\"\"\"\n\n        # STEP 1: Open the season\n        self.assertEqual(self.season.state, \"draft\")\n        self.season.action_open()\n        self.assertEqual(self.season.state, \"open\")\n\n        # STEP 2: Create a draft registration\n        reg = self.env[\"federation.season.registration\"].create(\n            {\n                \"season_id\": self.season.id,\n                \"team_id\": self.team.id,\n            }\n        )\n        self.assertEqual(reg.state, \"draft\")\n        self.assertTrue(reg.name)\n        self.assertNotEqual(reg.name, \"New\")\n\n        # STEP 3: Confirm\n        reg.action_confirm()\n        self.assertEqual(reg.state, \"confirmed\")\n\n        # STEP 4: Cancel\n        reg.action_cancel()\n        self.assertEqual(reg.state, \"cancelled\")\n\n        # STEP 5: Return to draft\n        reg.action_draft()\n        self.assertEqual(reg.state, \"draft\")\n\n        # STEP 6: Re-confirm\n        reg.action_confirm()\n        self.assertEqual(reg.state, \"confirmed\")\n\n        # Install the module with demo data enabled in Odoo.\n2. Open core workflows from tournament setup through match completion.\n3. Reset by recreating the database when you need a fresh deterministic dataset.\n\nScope and constraints\n- This module is for non-production environments.\n- Demo records prioritize workflow coverage over real-world data volume.\n- The seeded Spring Cup intentionally omits category and gender restrictions\n  because its participant fixture demonstrates senior, youth, and women's\n  teams in one navigable competition.\n- Data is versioned with the addon and intended to stay reproducible across installs.\n\nValidation checklist\n- Module installs without additional manual data steps.\n- Tournament, roster, and standings flows are navigable from seeded records.\n- Portal/public pages can render at least one realistic competition storyline.\n\n## Release-pilot qualification\n\nThe integrated P0-P3 release path is documented in\n`docs/RELEASE_PILOT_SCENARIO.md`. +{"content": "# sports_federation_demo\n\nDeterministic demo-data pack for the Sports Federation Odoo addons.\n\nPurpose\n- Seed a development or showcase database with realistic federation records.\n- Provide a repeatable baseline for onboarding, manual QA, and guided product demos.\n\nWhat it loads\n- Clubs, teams, players, seasons, registrations, tournaments, participants, and matches.\n- Supporting records used by rosters, standings, compliance, discipline, and notifications.\n\nHow to use\n1. Install the module with demo data enabled in Odoo.\n2. Open core workflows from tournament setup through match completion.\n3. Reset by recreating the database when you need a fresh deterministic dataset.\n\nScope and constraints\n- This module is for non-production environments.\n- Demo records prioritize workflow coverage over real-world data volume.\n- The seeded Spring Cup intentionally omits category and gender restrictions\n  because its participant fixture demonstrates senior, youth, and women's\n  teams in one navigable competition.\n- Data is versioned with the addon and intended to stay reproducible across installs.\n\nValidation checklist\n- Module installs without additional manual data steps.\n- Tournament, roster, and standings flows are navigable from seeded records.\n- Portal/public pages can render at least one realistic competition storyline.\n\n## Release-pilot qualification\n\nThe integrated P0-P3 release path is documented in\n`docs/RELEASE_PILOT_SCENARIO.md`. Run\n`scripts/ci/run_rc_validation.sh focus` before promoting a release candidate.\n\n## Accessible operator training walkthrough\n\nUse the deterministic demo database with a federation manager who also has the\ncompetition administrator, registration manager, competition designer, calendar\nplanner, schedule planner, schedule approver, and match-day manager roles.\n\nComplete the normal path without a mouse:\n\n1. Open **Competition Overview** and move through controls with `Tab` and\n   `Shift+Tab`. The focused control must always have a visible outline.\n2. Continue through **Registration**, **Format**, **Calendar**, **Schedule**, and\n   **Review**, activating links and buttons with `Enter` or `Space`.\n3. Submit an incomplete portal form. Focus must move to the error summary, and\n   invalid controls must reference that summary through `aria-describedby`.\n4. Open and close a confirmation modal. Focus must enter the modal and return to\n   the control that opened it.\n5. Repeat the club portal's competition, roster, officiating, and result tasks at\n   a 390 CSS-pixel viewport. No primary action may require horizontal page\n   scrolling.\n6. Confirm that every state is expressed as readable text. Color and icons may\n   reinforce the state but must not be the only status signal.\n\nThe `keyboard_competition_setup` browser tour qualifies the canonical backend\njourney mechanically. Portal template accessibility and mobile tests qualify\nlabels, error semantics, status text, responsive tables, and action wrapping.\n", "content_length": 2969, "filesystem_mode": "0644", "git_mode": "100644", "git_state": "tracked", "path": "sports_federation_demo/README.md", "record_type": "file", "sha256": "c135482f0b4d180a6b56d126cc1cb96d95e7e81cce8c339291b086ff9f5e09da", "size_bytes": 2969}

 {"content": "# Sports Federation Discipline\n\nIncident reporting, disciplinary case management, sanctions, and player\nsuspensions. Provides the full disciplinary pipeline from incident detection\nthrough case resolution to sanction enforcement.\n\n## Purpose\n\nTracks **incidents** that occur during matches, groups them into **disciplinary\ncases** for investigation, and records **sanctions** (fines, warnings) and\n**suspensions** (match bans) as outcomes. Links back to players, clubs, and\nreferees for comprehensive disciplinary records.\n\n## Dependencies\n\n| Module | Reason |\n|--------|--------|\n| `sports_federation_base` | Clubs |\n| `sports_federation_people` | Players |\n| `sports_federation_tournament` | Matches |\n| `mail` | Chatter |\n\n## Models\n\n### `federation.match.incident`\n\nAn event that occurred during a match that may trigger disciplinary action.\n\n| Field | Type | Description |\n|-------|------|-------------|\n| `name` | Char | Incident title |\n| `match_id` | Many2one | The match |\n| `player_id` | Many2one | Involved player |\n| `club_id` | Many2one | Involved club |\n| `referee_id` | Many2one | Reporting referee |\n| `incident_type` | Selection | warning / yellow_card / red_card / misconduct / violence / admin_issue / other |\n| `minute_text` | Char | Match minute (text for flexibility) |\n| `description` | Text | What happened |\n| `case_id` | Many2one | Linked disciplinary case |\n| `date_reported` | Date | When reported |\n| `reported_by_user_id` | Many2one | System user who filed it |\n| `status` | Selection | new / attached / closed |\n\n### `federation.disciplinary.case`\n\nAn investigation triggered by one or more incidents.\n\n| Field | Type | Description |\n|-------|------|-------------|\n| `name` | Char | Case title |\n| `reference` | Char | Auto-sequence reference number |\n| `state` | Selection | draft / under_review / decided / appealed / closed |\n| `opened_on` / `decided_on` / `closed_on` | Date | Timeline |\n| `responsible_user_id` | Many2one | Case handler |\n| `incident_ids` | One2many | Related incidents |\n| `sanction_ids` | One2many | Resulting sanctions |\n| `suspension_ids` | One2many | Resulting suspensions |\n| `incident_count` / `sanction_count` / `suspension_count` | Integer | Dedicated stat counters for case shortcuts |\n| `subject_player_id` / `subject_club_id` / `subject_referee_id` | Many2one | Subject |\n| `summary` / `notes` | Text | Case details |\n\n- **State machine**: draft → under_review → decided → appealed / closed, with under-review cases allowed back to draft for corrections before a decision is recorded.\n- **Auto-numbering** via `ir.sequence`.\n- **Counter labels** use distinct technical field names so the case relation tabs and their stat counters do not trigger duplicate-label warnings during module loading.\n\n### `federation.sanction`\n\nA penalty imposed as the outcome of a disciplinary case.\n\n| Field | Type | Description |\n|-------|------|-------------|\n| `name` | Char | Sanction title |\n| `case_id` | Many2one | Parent case |\n| `sanction_type` | Selection | fine / warning / ban / point_deduction / other |\n| `player_id` / `club_id` / `referee_id` | Many2one | Who is sanctioned |\n| `amount` / `currency_id` | Monetary | For fines |\n| `effective_date` | Date | When it takes effect |\n| `notes` | Text | Details |\n\n### `federation.suspension`\n\nA time-bound match ban for a player.\n\n| Field | Type | Description |\n|-------|------|-------------|\n| `name` | Char | Suspension title |\n| `case_id` | Many2one | Parent case |\n| `player_id` | Many2one | Suspended player |\n| `date_start` / `date_end` | Date | Ban period |\n| `state` | Selection | active / served / overturned |\n| `notes` | Text | Details |\n\n## Security Groups\n\n| Group | Purpose |\n|-------|---------|\n| Disciplinary Staff | Can manage incidents, cases, sanctions, and suspensions |\n\n## Data Files\n\n| File | Content |\n|------|---------|\n| `data/ir_sequence.xml` | `FED-DISC-` sequence for case reference numbers |\n\n## Key Behaviours\n\n1. **Incident → Case linking** — Incidents can be grouped into a case for joint handling.\n2. **Case lifecycle** — draft → under_review → decided → appealed / closed with date tracking, and under-review cases can be reopened to draft before a decision is recorded.\n3. **Multiple outcomes** — A single case can produce multiple sanctions and suspensions.\n4. **Player integration** — Inherited views add discipline tabs to player forms.\n5. **Match integration** — Inherited views add incident lists to match forms.\n6. **Finance bridge integration** — When `sports_federation_finance_bridge` is installed,\n   sanctions of type `fine` automatically create source-linked finance events and\n   keep draft amounts synchronized with sanction edits.\n", "content_length": 4687, "filesystem_mode": "0644", "git_mode": "100644", "git_state": "tracked", "path": -{"content": "# Sports Federation Club Roles and Portal\n\nVersion: 19.0.4.1.0\nOwner: Federation Platform Team\nLast reviewed: 2026-08-23\nReview cadence: Every release\n\nRole-oriented self-service portal for club representatives, officials, and tournament operators.\n\n## Primary user journeys\n\n### Club representative\n\n- manage club teams and players\n- submit season and tournament registrations\n- prepare and activate rosters\n- complete match sheets and readiness tasks\n- nominate officials\n- review result actions and deadlines\n\n### Official\n\n- view assignments\n- confirm or decline within the authorized scope\n- access relevant match information\n\n### Tournament operator\n\n- use the tournament operations board\n- monitor courts, matches, readiness, and action queues\n- execute scoped result and operational actions\n\n## Trust boundary\n\nAll privileged portal writes must use a model-owned method and the shared `federation.portal.privilege` boundary. Controllers validate input and resolve scope but do not perform raw elevated business writes. See `adr/0001-portal-trust-boundaries.md`.\n\nEvery new privileged route requires:\n\n1. positive in-scope coverage\n2. negative out-of-scope coverage\n3. direct-ID or guessed-record coverage\n4. model/helper ownership assertions when elevation is used\n5. CSRF enforcement for state-changing browser or JSON-RPC routes\n\n## Portal UX direction\n\nThe landing page should remain task-first. Common tasks are teams, registrations, upcoming matches, and action items. Specialist tools should be progressively disclosed instead of presented as an undifferentiated module catalogue.\n\n## Operations board\n\nThe board loads through scoped JSON-RPC, supports polling and structured failures, and exposes only matches and actions available to the current user. Server-side checks remain authoritative.\n\n## Tests\n\nThe module includes HTTP smoke, ownership, access-denied, team, player, roster, registration, officiating, result, accessibility, mobile, operations-board, and end-to-end portal workflow tests.\n\n## Consolidated documentation\n\n`PORTAL_OWNERSHIP_COVERAGE.md`, `PORTAL_OWNERSHIP_TEST_MATRIX.md`, and `ROADMAP_RC.md` are deleted. Their durable rules are consolidated here and in ADR-0001; the test files are the authoritative live coverage map.\n\n## competition portal\n\nThe active competition portal is edition-based. Participation comes from\ncompetition entries and finalized participant sets. Match-day schedule data is\nrendered only from each match day's current live publication. Draft schedules,\nreview evidence and superseded publications are intentionally unavailable.\n\nPrimary routes:\n\n- `/my/competitions`\n- `/my/competition-entries`\n- `/my/match-days`\n- `/sports/match-days/<id>/operations`\n\nLegacy tournament workspace routes are redirect-only and render no V1 data.\nThe backend tournament-registration action is loaded before the menus that use\nit, so a fresh module install and an upgrade both build the registration menu\nwithout relying on stale database metadata.\n", "content_length": 2994, "filesystem_mode": "0644", "git_mode": "100644", "git_state": "tracked", "path": "sports_federation_portal/README.md", "record_type": "file", "sha256": "9ff77294460c25ba8798e5b29ecdb05ce6d12c1d7d25ea2bba2462d44b3b9e05", "size_bytes": 2994}
+{"content": "# Sports Federation Club Roles and Portal\n\nVersion: 19.0.4.1.0\nOwner: Federation Platform Team\nLast reviewed: 2026-08-23\nReview cadence: Every release\n\nRole-oriented self-service portal for club representatives, officials, and tournament operators.\n\n## Primary user journeys\n\n### Club representative\n\n- manage club teams and players\n- submit season and tournament registrations\n- prepare and activate rosters\n- complete match sheets and readiness tasks\n- nominate officials\n- review result actions and deadlines\n\n### Official\n\n- view assignments\n- confirm or decline within the authorized scope\n- access relevant match information\n\n### Tournament operator\n\n- use the tournament operations board\n- monitor courts, matches, readiness, and action queues\n- execute scoped result and operational actions\n\n## Trust boundary\n\nAll privileged portal writes must use a model-owned method and the shared `federation.portal.privilege` boundary. Controllers validate input and resolve scope but do not perform raw elevated business writes. See `adr/0001-portal-trust-boundaries.md`.\n\nEvery new privileged route requires:\n\n1. positive in-scope coverage\n2. negative out-of-scope coverage\n3. direct-ID or guessed-record coverage\n4. model/helper ownership assertions when elevation is used\n5. CSRF enforcement for state-changing browser or JSON-RPC routes\n\n## Portal UX direction\n\nThe landing page should remain task-first. Common tasks are teams, registrations, upcoming matches, and action items. Specialist tools should be progressively disclosed instead of presented as an undifferentiated module catalogue.\n\n## Operations board\n\nThe board loads through scoped JSON-RPC, supports polling and structured failures, and exposes only matches and actions available to the current user. Server-side checks remain authoritative.\n\n## Tests\n\nThe module includes HTTP smoke, ownership, access-denied, team, player, roster, registration, officiating, result, accessibility, mobile, operations-board, and end-to-end portal workflow tests.\n\n## Consolidated documentation\n\n`PORTAL_OWNERSHIP_COVERAGE.md`, `PORTAL_OWNERSHIP_TEST_MATRIX.md`, and `ROADMAP_RC.md` are deleted. Their durable rules are consolidated here and in ADR-0001; the test files are the authoritative live coverage map.\n\n## competition portal\n\nThe active competition portal is edition-based. Participation comes from\ncompetition entries and finalized participant sets. Match-day schedule data is\nrendered only from each match day's current live publication. Draft schedules,\nreview evidence and superseded publications are intentionally unavailable.\n\nPrimary routes:\n\n- `/my/competitions`\n- `/my/competition-entries`\n- `/my/match-days`\n- `/sports/match-days/<id>/operations`\n\nLegacy tournament workspace routes are redirect-only and render no V1 data.\nThe backend tournament-registration action is loaded before the menus that use\nit, so a fresh module install and an upgrade both build the registration menu\nwithout relying on stale database metadata.\n\n\n## Accessible error recovery and focus management\n\nPortal forms move keyboard focus to server-rendered error summaries, connect\ninvalid controls to that summary with `aria-describedby`, and announce the\nsummary through an assertive live region. Bootstrap confirmation dialogs move\nfocus into the dialog and restore it to the invoking control when closed.\nKeyboard focus uses a visible outline that does not depend on color-only status\ncues. The focused accessibility suite covers these contracts together with\nmobile table and action wrapping.\n", "content_length": 3540, "filesystem_mode": "0644", "git_mode": "100644", "git_state": "tracked", "path": "sports_federation_portal/README.md", "record_type": "file", "sha256": "3ea4f6e8c7c0686f04a9a5a78e3b76b66861847771f9655441258519bd4016a5", "size_bytes": 3540}

[\"/my/competitions/<int:edition_id>\"],\n        type=\"http\",\n        auth=\"user\",\n        website=True,\n    )\n    def portal_my_competition_detail(self, edition_id, **kw):\n        try:\n portal_create_competition_entry(self, window_id, team_id, **kw):\n        try:\n            window = (\n                request.env[\"federation.registration.window\"]\n                .sudo()\n                .browse(int(window_id))\n                .exists()\n            )\n            team = request.env[\"federation.team\"].sudo().browse(int(team_id)).exists()\n            request.env[\"federation.portal.scope\"].assert_team(\n                team, user=request.env.user\n            )\n            if not window or window.state != \"open\":\n                raise ValidationError(_(\"The registration window is not open.\"))\n            if window.division_id.get_team_eligibility_error(team):\n                raise ValidationError(\n                    window.division_id.get_team_eligibility_error(team)\n                )\n            entry = request.env[\"federation.portal.privilege\"].portal_create(\n csrf=True,\n    )\n    def portal_matchday_action(self, matchday_id, match_id, **kw):\n        try:\n            workspace = request.env[\"federation.portal.matchday.queries\"].detail(\n raise AccessError()\n            privilege = request.env[\"federation.portal.privilege\"]\n            action = kw.get(\"action\")\n            if action == \"start\":\n                privilege.portal_call(match, \"action_start\", user=request.env.user)\n            elif action == \"finish\":\n                privilege.portal_call(match, \"action_done\", user=request.env.user)\n raise ValidationError(_(\"Unsupported match-day action.\"))\n        except (AccessError, ValidationError, ValueError) as exc:\n            return {\n                \"ok\": False,\n  {"content": "from odoo.tests.common import TransactionCase\nfrom odoo.exceptions import ValidationError\nfrom datetime import date, timedelta\nfrom odoo.tools.misc import mute_logger\n\n\nclass TestClubRepresentative(TransactionCase):\n    \"\"\"Tests for club representative model and role types.\"\"\"\n\n    @classmethod\n    def setUpClass(cls):\n        \"\"\"Set up shared test data for the test case.\"\"\"\n        super().setUpClass()\n        # Create a club\n        cls.club = cls.env[\"federation.club\"].create({\"name\": \"Test Club\"})\n        cls.user = (\n            cls.env[\"res.users\"]\n            .with_context(no_reset_password=True)\n            .create(\n                {\n                    \"name\": \"Club Representative Test User\",\n                    \"login\": \"club.representative.test.user@example.com\",\n                    \"email\": \"club.representative.test.user@example.com\",\n                }\n            )\n        )\n        # Create partners\n        cls.partner1 = cls.env[\"res.partner\"].create({\"name\": \"John Doe\"})\n        cls.partner2 = cls.env[\"res.partner\"].create({\"name\": \"Jane Smith\"})\n        cls.partner3 = cls.env[\"res.partner\"].create({\"name\": \"Bob Johnson\"})\n        # Get role types (created by data file)\n        cls.role_competition = cls.env.ref(\n            \"sports_federation_portal.role_type_competition_contact\"\n        )\n        cls.role_finance = cls.env.ref(\n            \"sports_federation_portal.role_type_finance_contact\"\n        )\n        cls.role_safeguarding = cls.env.ref(\n            \"sports_federation_portal.role_type_safeguarding_contact\"\n        )\n        cls.role_president = cls.env.ref(\"sports_federation_portal.role_type_president\")\n        cls.role_other = cls.env.ref(\"sports_federation_portal.role_type_other\")\n\n    def test_create_multiple_representatives(self):\n        \"\"\"Test creating multiple representatives for one club.\"\"\"\n        rep1 = self.env[\"federation.club.representative\"].create(\n            {\n                \"club_id\": self.club.id,\n                \"partner_id\": self.partner1.id,\n                \"role_type_id\": self.role_competition.id,\n                \"is_primary\": True,\n                \"date_start\": \"2025-01-01\",\n            }\n        )\n        rep2 = self.env[\"federation.club.representative\"].create(\n            {\n                \"club_id\": self.club.id,\n                \"partner_id\": self.partner2.id,\n                \"role_type_id\": self.role_finance.id,\n                \"is_primary\": True,\n                \"date_start\": \"2025-01-01\",\n            }\n        )\n        self.assertEqual(len(self.club.representative_ids), 2)\n        self.assertIn(rep1, self.club.representative_ids)\n        self.assertIn(rep2, self.club.representative_ids)\n\n    def test_role_type_linkage(self):\n        \"\"\"Test that representative correctly links to role type.\"\"\"\n        rep = self.env[\"federation.club.representative\"].create(\n            {\n                \"club_id\": self.club.id,\n                \"partner_id\": self.partner1.id,\n                \"role_type_id\": self.role_competition.id,\n            }\n        )\n        self.assertEqual(rep.role_type_id, self.role_competition)\n        self.assertTrue(rep.role_type_id.is_competition_contact)\n        self.assertFalse(rep.role_type_id.is_finance_contact)\n\n    def test_primary_contact_uniqueness(self):\n        \"\"\"Test that only one primary representative per role type per club.\"\"\"\n        self.env[\"federation.club.representative\"].create(\n            {\n                \"club_id\": self.club.id,\n                \"partner_id\": self.partner1.id,\n                \"role_type_id\": self.role_competition.id,\n                \"is_primary\": True,\n            }\n        )\n        # Attempt to create another primary for same role type should fail\n        with self.assertRaises(ValidationError):\n            self.env[\"federation.club.representative\"].create(\n                {\n                    \"club_id\": self.club.id,\n                    \"partner_id\": self.partner2.id,\n                    \"role_type_id\": self.role_competition.id,\n                    \"is_primary\": True,\n                }\n            )\n\n    def test_multiple_primaries_different_roles(self):\n        \"\"\"Test that different role types can each have a primary.\"\"\"\n        rep1 = self.env[\"federation.club.representative\"].create(\n            {\n                \"club_id\": self.club.id,\n                \"partner_id\": self.partner1.id,\n                \"role_type_id\": self.role_competition.id,\n                \"is_primary\": True,\n            }\n        )\n        rep2 = self.env[\"federation.club.representative\"].create(\n            {\n                \"club_id\": self.club.id,\n                \"partner_id\": self.partner2.id,\n                \"role_type_id\": self.role_finance.id,\n                \"is_primary\": True,\n            }\n        )\n        self.assertTrue(rep1.is_primary)\n        self.assertTrue(rep2.is_primary)\n\n    def test_is_current_computation(self):\n        \"\"\"Test is_current computed field.\"\"\"\n        # Active representative with no dates\n        rep = self.env[\"federation.club.representative\"].create(\n            {\n                \"club_id\": self.club.id,\n                \"partner_id\": self.partner1.id,\n                \"role_type_id\": self.role_competition.id,\n            }\n        )\n        self.assertTrue(rep.is_current)\n\n        # Representative with start date in future\n        rep.date_start = date.today() + timedelta(days=30)\n        rep._compute_is_current()\n        self.assertFalse(rep.is_current)\n\n        # Representative with end date in past\n        rep.date_start = date.today() - timedelta(days=60)\n        rep.date_end = date.today() - timedelta(days=1)\n        rep._compute_is_current()\n        self.assertFalse(rep.is_current)\n\n        # Representative with valid date range\n        rep.date_start = date.today() - timedelta(days=30)\n        rep.date_end = date.today() + timedelta(days=30)\n        rep._compute_is_current()\n        self.assertTrue(rep.is_current)\n\n    def test_date_validation(self):\n        \"\"\"Test that start date cannot be after end date.\"\"\"\n        with self.assertRaises(ValidationError):\n            self.env[\"federation.club.representative\"].create(\n                {\n                    \"club_id\": self.club.id,\n                    \"partner_id\": self.partner1.id,\n                    \"role_type_id\": self.role_competition.id,\n                    \"date_start\": \"2025-12-31\",\n                    \"date_end\": \"2025-01-01\",\n                }\n            )\n\n    def test_partner_club_role_uniqueness(self):\n        \"\"\"Test that a partner can only have one representative per club and role.\"\"\"\n        self.env[\"federation.club.representative\"].create(\n            {\n                \"club_id\": self.club.id,\n                \"partner_id\": self.partner1.id,\n                \"role_type_id\": self.role_competition.id,\n            }\n        )\n        # Same partner, same club, same role should fail\n        with self.assertRaises(Exception), mute_logger(\n            \"odoo.sql_db\"\n        ), self.cr.savepoint():\n            self.env[\"federation.club.representative\"].create(\n                {\n                    \"club_id\": self.club.id,\n                    \"partner_id\": self.partner1.id,\n                    \"role_type_id\": self.role_competition.id,\n                }\n            )\n\n    def test_get_club_for_user(self):\n        \"\"\"Test _get_club_for_user helper method.\"\"\"\n        # No representative yet\n        club = self.env[\"federation.club.representative\"]._get_club_for_user(self.user)\n        self.assertFalse(club)\n\n        # Create representative linked to user\n  {"content": "import re\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom lxml import etree\n\nfrom odoo.tests.common import TransactionCase, tagged\n\nMIN_COLUMNS_FOR_RESPONSIVE = 5\n\n\n@tagged(\"-at_install\", \"post_install\", \"sf_frontend_mobile\")\nclass TestPortalTemplateMobile(TransactionCase):\n    \"\"\"Verify mobile-specific layout conventions in the largest portal templates.\n\n    These checks complement the accessibility tests by validating that wide\n    tables are horizontally scrollable, that action-button groups wrap on\n    narrow viewports, and that no inline width constraints cause horizontal\n    overflow on phone-sized screens.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    @classmethod\n    def setUpClass(cls):\n        super().setUpClass()\n        views_dir = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / \"views\"\n        cls.template_roots = {\n            \"portal_templates\": etree.parse(\n                str(views_dir / \"portal_templates.xml\")\n            ).getroot(),\n            \"portal_roster_templates\": etree.parse(\n                str(views_dir / \"portal_roster_templates.xml\")\n            ).getroot(),\n            \"competition_templates\": etree.parse(\n                str(views_dir / \"competition_templates.xml\")\n            ).getroot(),\n            \"portal_officiating_templates\": etree.parse(\n                str(views_dir / \"portal_officiating_templates.xml\")\n            ).getroot(),\n        }\n\n    def _template(self, file_key, template_id):\n        templates = self.template_roots[file_key].xpath(\n            f\".//template[@id='{template_id}']\"\n        )\n        self.assertTrue(\n            templates, f\"Template {template_id} should exist in {file_key}.\"\n        )\n        return templates[0]\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # Assertion helpers\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    def _assert_wide_tables_wrapped_responsive(self, file_key, template_id):\n        \"\"\"Tables with MIN_COLUMNS_FOR_RESPONSIVE+ header columns must live\n        inside a ``table-responsive`` ancestor so they scroll on narrow screens.\n        \"\"\"\n        template = self._template(file_key, template_id)\n        tables = template.xpath(\".//table[.//thead]\")\n        for table in tables:\n            col_count = len(table.xpath(\".//thead//th\"))\n            if col_count < MIN_COLUMNS_FOR_RESPONSIVE:\n                continue\n            # Walk up to find a table-responsive wrapper\n            parent = table.getparent()\n            found = False\n            while parent is not None:\n                classes = parent.get(\"class\", \"\")\n                if \"table-responsive\" in classes.split():\n                    found = True\n                    break\n                parent = parent.getparent()\n            self.assertTrue(\n                found,\n                f\"Table with {col_count} columns in {template_id} \"\n                f\"should be wrapped in a table-responsive container.\",\n            )\n\n    def _assert_action_groups_wrap(self, file_key, template_id):\n        \"\"\"Button groups using ``d-flex`` should include ``flex-wrap`` so\n        action buttons stack on phone-width viewports instead of overflowing.\n        \"\"\"\n        template = self._template(file_key, template_id)\n        groups = template.xpath(\n            \".//*[contains(concat(' ', normalize-space(@class), ' '), ' d-flex ')]\"\n        )\n        for group in groups:\n            classes = group.get(\"class\", \"\").split()\n            if \"d-flex\" not in classes:\n                continue\n            # Only check groups that contain buttons or links\n            has_actions = group.xpath(\"./*[self::button or self::a or self::form]\")\n            if not has_actions:\n                continue\n            self.assertIn(\n                \"flex-wrap\",\n                classes,\n                f\"Action group in {template_id} uses d-flex but is missing \"\n                f\"flex-wrap for mobile overflow.\",\n            )\n\n    def _assert_no_table_fixed_width_overflow(self, file_key, template_id):\n        \"\"\"Data tables (with <thead>) should not carry inline ``width`` styles\n        on the <table> element itself, as this can force horizontal overflow\n        on narrow viewports.\n        \"\"\"\n        template = self._template(file_key, template_id)\n        tables = template.xpath(\".//table[.//thead]\")\n        for table in tables:\n            style = table.get(\"style\", \"\")\n            self.assertFalse(\n                re.search(r\"width\\s*:\", style),\n                f\"Data table in {template_id} has an inline width style \"\n                f\"that may cause mobile overflow.\",\n            )\n\n    # -{"content": "# Sports Federation Reporting\n\nCross-module analytical reports backed by PostgreSQL views plus scheduled report\nsnapshots. Provides read-only report models that aggregate participation,\nofficiating, compliance, finance, and tournament-readiness data into\noperator-facing views.\n\n## Purpose\n\nGives federation administrators a **dashboard-level view** of key metrics without\nwriting SQL or building custom reports. Each report model is a database view\nthat joins and aggregates data from multiple modules into a single, filterable\nlist or pivot view, while report schedules generate recurring weekly or monthly\nCSV snapshots from inside Odoo.\n\n## Dependencies\n\n| Module | Reason |\n|--------|--------|\n| `sports_federation_base` | Clubs, teams, seasons |\n| `sports_federation_people` | Players |\n| `sports_federation_tournament` | Tournaments |\n| `sports_federation_officiating` | Referees, assignments |\n| `sports_federation_standings` | Standings data |\n| `sports_federation_discipline` | Disciplinary data |\n| `sports_federation_compliance` | Compliance data |\n| `sports_federation_finance_bridge` | Finance events |\n| `sports_federation_import_tools` | Inbound delivery failures for operator checklist reporting |\n\nAudit dashboard surfaces:\n\n- `Reporting > Portal Audit` groups privileged portal create/write/call activity\n\temitted through `federation.portal.privilege`.\n- `Reporting > Token Rotation Audit` groups manager-driven integration partner\n\ttoken rotation events emitted from the import-tools token rotation flow.\n- both dashboards read the shared `federation.audit.event` log so additional\n\taudited workflows can reuse the same reporting contract later.\n\n## Models (all SQL view-backed, `_auto = False`)\n\n### `federation.report.participation`\n\nClub participation summary per season.\n\n| Field | Type | Description |\n|-------|------|-------------|\n| `season_id` | Many2one | Season |\n| `club_id` | Many2one | Club |\n| `team_count` | Integer | Teams registered |\n| `player_count` | Integer | Players licensed |\n| `tournament_count` | Integer | Tournaments entered |\n\n### `federation.report.officiating`\n\nReferee workload summary.\n\n| Field | Type | Description |\n|-------|------|-------------|\n| `referee_id` | Many2one | Referee |\n| `certification_level` | Char | Current level |\n| `assignment_count` | Integer | Total assignments |\n| `completed_assignment_count` | Integer | Completed assignments |\n\n### `federation.report.compliance`\n\nCompliance status overview by entity type.\n\n| Field | Type | Description |\n|-------|------|-------------|\n| `target_model` | Char | Entity type |\n| `compliant_count` | Integer | Entities in compliance |\n| `missing_count` | Integer | Missing documents |\n| `pending_count` | Integer | Awaiting review |\n| `expired_count` | Integer | Expired documents |\n| `non_compliant_count` | Integer | Explicitly rejected or non-compliant checks |\n\n### `federation.report.finance`\n\nFinancial event summary by fee type and state.\n\n| Field | Type | Description |\n|-------|------|-------------|\n| `fee_type_id` | Many2one | Fee category |\n| `state` | Selection | Event state |\n| `event_count` | Integer | Number of events |\n| `total_amount` | Float | Sum of amounts |\n\n### `federation.report.operational`\n\nTournament-level operational KPI view.\n\n| Field | Type | Description |\n|-------|------|-------------|\n| `season_id` / `tournament_id` | Many2one | Scope of the KPI row |\n| `participant_count` / `confirmed_participant_count` | Integer | Enrolment totals |\n| `participant_confirmation_rate` | Float | Confirmed-participant percentage |\n| `match_count` / `completed_match_count` | Integer | Match execution totals |\n| `match_completion_rate` | Float | Completed-match percentage |\n| `pending_finance_event_count` / `pending_finance_amount` | Integer / Float | Open finance follow-up tied to match operations |\n| `open_club_compliance_count` | Integer | Outstanding club compliance checks for participating clubs |\n| `readiness_status` | Selection | `healthy`, `attention`, or `blocked` |\n| `readiness_note` | Text | Operator-readable summary of the active readiness blockers or follow-up work |\n\n### `federation.report.standing.reconciliation`\n\nTournament-level standings coverage check.\n\n| Field | Type | Description |\n|-------|------|-------------|\n| `confirmed_participant_count` | Integer | Confirmed tournament participants |\n| `covered_participant_count` | Integer | Distinct participants represented in standings |\n| `missing_participant_count` | Integer | Confirmed participants missing from standings coverage |\n| `orphaned_participant_count` | Integer | Standings entries without a matching confirmed participant |\n| `reconciliation_status` | Selection | `healthy`, `attention`, or `blocked` |\n| `reconciliation_note` | Text | Operator-readable mismatch explanation |\n\n### `federation.report.finance.reconciliation`\n\nFinance follow-up queue by event.\n\n| Field | Type | Description |\n|-------|------|-------------|\n| `finance_event_id` | Many2one | Source finance event |\n| `counterparty_display` | Char | Club, player, referee, or partner name |\n| `follow_up_status` | Selection | Draft / awaiting settlement / awaiting reference / complete / cancelled |\n| `needs_follow_up` | Boolean | Whether the event still needs operator attention |\n| `age_days` | Integer | Days since the event was created |\n| `invoice_ref` / `external_ref` | Char | Reconciliation references |\n\n### `federation.report.notification.exception`\n\nFailed-notification queue backed by the notification log.\n\n| Field | Type | Description |\n|-------|------|-------------|\n| `notification_log_id` | `success`, or `failed` |\n| `last_failure_on` / `last_error_message` | Datetime / Text | Persisted failure state for the most recent failed generation |\n| `consecutive_failure_count` | Integer | Number of failed generation attempts in a row |\n| `generated_file` | Binary | Last generated CSV snapshot |\n| `last_row_count` | Integer | Number of exported data rows |\n\nImplementation note:\n\n- Report-type-specific row builders and back-office action metadata now live in `services/report_schedule_builders.py`.\n- Keep new schedule types in that registry so `federation.report.schedule` stays `federation.report.operator.checklist`\n\nOperator-facing queue roll-up that consolidates the release-critical exception\nsurfaces into a single reporting menu.\n\n| Field | Type | Description |\n|-------|------|-------------|\n| `queue_code` / `queue_name` | Selection / Char | Stable identifier and display name for the queue |\n| `owner_display` | Char | Operational owner for the queue |\n| `open_count` / `escalated_count` | Integer | Queue depth and escalated subset |\n| `oldest_age_days` | Integer | Oldest unresolved item age |\n| `status` | Selection | `healthy`, `attention`, or `blocked` |\n| `summary` | Text | Operator-readable explanation of what the queue means |\n\n## Key Behaviours\n\n1. **Read-only views** — Analytical models use `_auto = False` with `init()` creating SQL views.\n2. **Role-oriented reporting surfaces** — Operational KPIs, standings reconciliation, finance reconciliation, and compliance summaries are separated into task-specific menus instead of a single generic export path.\n3. **Cross-module joins** — Operational KPIs combine tournament, standings, finance, and compliance data into one application-layer view.\n4. **Recurring snapshots** — `federation.report.schedule` can generate weekly or monthly CSV snapshots and a daily cron refreshes active schedules.\n5. **Reconciliation-first reporting** — Standings coverage, finance follow-up, failed-notification queues, missing discipline-finance events, and stalled workflow queues expose the specific gaps operators must resolve before relying on downstream outputs.\n6. **Season operations checklist** — The reporting layer now includes a season-level checklist that surfaces review queues, publication gaps, and unresolved workflow exceptions in one place.\n7. **Planning decision support** — Season portfolio and club performance views turn the reporting layer into a planning and delivery-monitoring surface instead of a pure export utility.\n8. **Legacy CSV exports preserved** — The lightweight HTTP CSV endpoints remain available for ad hoc export use.\n9. **Contract-tagged exports** — Authenticated CSV responses now include explicit contract and version headers for downstream consumers.\n10. **Operator checklist** — Notification failures, workflow exceptions, finance follow-up, inbound delivery failures, blocked season readiness, and scheduled report failures now surface in one menu.\n11. **Persistent schedule failure tracking** — Report schedules keep the last failure timestamp, error message, and consecutive failure count so recurring problems survive beyond transient logs.\n12. **Generated-file retention** — Scheduled report artifacts are cleared automatically after the retention window in `DATA_RETENTION_POLICY.md`, while the schedule metadata and next run remain intact.\n\n## CSV exports\n\nAuthenticated backend users can export lightweight KPI CSV files from the\nreporting controllers:\n\n- `/reporting/export/standings/<tournament_id>` — standings lines with tie-break notes\n- `/reporting/export/participation/<season_id>` — season participation roster\n- `/reporting/export/finance` — finance summary grouped by fee type and state\n- `/reporting/export/finance/events` — detailed finance-event handoff export with reconciliation references and closure timestamps\n\nEach export now exposes `X-Federation-Contract` and\n`X-Federation-Contract-Version` headers. The detailed finance-event handoff\nexport uses the `finance_event_v1` contract.\n\nFor recurring in-application reporting, create records under **Federation → Reporting → Report Schedules**.\nEach schedule stores the last generated CSV snapshot directly on the schedule record.\nUse **Federation → Reporting → Operator Checklist** for the release-readiness\nqueue view that links back to the underlying exception surfaces.\n", "content_length": 15828, "filesystem_mode": "0644", "git_mode": "100644", "git_state": "tracked", "path": "sports_federation_reporting/README.md", "record_type": "file", "sha256": "74a20b38e6cb4b156489e215d77a45ee51368d6a217bbd444d4e961119b2545a", "size_bytes": 15868}
+{"content": "# Sports Federation Reporting\n\nCross-module analytical reports backed by PostgreSQL views plus scheduled report\nsnapshots. Provides read-only report models that aggregate participation,\nofficiating, compliance, finance, and tournament-readiness data into\noperator-facing views.\n\n## Purpose\n\nGives federation administrators a **dashboard-level view** of key metrics without\nwriting SQL or building custom reports. Each report model is a database view\nthat joins and aggregates data from multiple modules into a single, filterable\nlist or pivot view, while report schedules generate recurring weekly or monthly\nCSV snapshots from inside Odoo.\n\n## Dependencies\n\n| Module | Reason |\n|--------|--------|\n| `sports_federation_base` | Clubs, teams, seasons |\n| `sports_federation_people` | Players |\n| `sports_federation_tournament` | Tournaments |\n| `sports_federation_officiating` | Referees, assignments |\n| `sports_federation_standings` | Standings data |\n| `sports_federation_discipline` | Disciplinary data |\n| `sports_federation_compliance` | Compliance data |\n| `sports_federation_finance_bridge` | Finance events |\n| `sports_federation_import_tools` | Inbound delivery failures for operator checklist reporting |\n\nAudit dashboard surfaces:\n\n- `Reporting > Portal Audit` groups privileged portal create/write/call activity\n\temitted through `federation.portal.privilege`.\n- `Reporting > Token Rotation Audit` groups manager-driven integration partner\n\ttoken rotation events emitted from the import-tools token rotation flow.\n- both dashboards read the shared `federation.audit.event` log so additional\n\taudited workflows can reuse the same reporting contract later.\n\n## Models (all SQL view-backed, `_auto = False`)\n\n### `federation.report.participation`\n\nClub participation summary per season.\n\n| Field | Type | Description |\n|-------|------|-------------|\n| `season_id` | Many2one | Season |\n| `club_id` | Many2one | Club |\n| `team_count` | Integer | Teams registered |\n| `player_count` | Integer | Players licensed |\n| `tournament_count` | Integer | Tournaments entered |\n\n### Current level |\n| `assignment_count` | Integer | Total assignments |\n| `completed_assignment_count` | Integer | Completed assignments |\n\n### `federation.report.compliance`\n\nCompliance status `missing_count` | Integer | Missing documents |\n| `pending_count` | Integer | Awaiting review |\n| `expired_count` | Integer | Expired documents |\n| `non_compliant_count` | Integer | Explicitly rejected or non-compliant checks |\n\n### `federation.report.finance`\n\nFinancial event summary by fee type and state.\n\n| Field | Type | Description |\n|-------|------|-------------|\n| KPI row |\n| `participant_count` / `confirmed_participant_count` | Integer | Enrolment totals |\n| `participant_confirmation_rate` | Float | Confirmed-participant percentage |\n| `match_count` / `completed_match_count` | Integer | Match execution totals |\n| `match_completion_rate` | Float | Completed-match percentage |\n| `pending_finance_event_count` / `pending_finance_amount` | Integer / Float | Open finance follow-up tied to match operations |\n| `open_club_compliance_count` | Integer | Outstanding club compliance checks for participating clubs |\n| `readiness_status` | Selection | `healthy`, `attention`, or `blocked` |\n| `readiness_note` | Text | Operator-readable summary of the active readiness blockers or follow-up work |\n\n###  {"content": "import logging\n\nfrom odoo import api, models, tools\n\n_logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n\nclass FederationReportSqlHelper(models.AbstractModel):\n    _name = \"federation.report.sql.helper\"\n    _description = \"Federation Reporting SQL Helper\"\n\n    @api.model\n    def recreate_view(self, table_name, sql_query):\n        \"\"\"Drop and recreate a SQL view with shared logging and consistent behavior.\"\"\"\n        tools.drop_view_if_exists(self.env.cr, table_name)\n        self.env.cr.execute(sql_query)\n\n    @api.model\n    def run_query(self, sql_query, params=None):\n        \"\"\"Execute parameterized SQL in one place for report service consistency.\"\"\"\n        self.env.cr.execute(sql_query, params or [])\n        return self.env.cr.fetchall()\n\n    @api.model\n    def run_explain(self, sql_query, params=None):\n        \"\"\"Run EXPLAIN on a report SQL query for troubleshooting and CI snapshots.\"\"\"\n        explain_query = f\"EXPLAIN {sql_query}\"\n        self.env.cr.execute(explain_query, params or [])\n        plan = self.env.cr.fetchall()\n        _logger.debug(\"EXPLAIN plan captured with %s line(s)\", len(plan))\n        return plan\n", "content_length": 1128, "filesystem_mode": "0644", "git_mode": "100644", "git_state": "tracked", "path": "sports_federation_reporting/models/report_sql_helper.py", "record_type": "file", "sha256": "e361a40d598d051a778a28d4d892a6424ee5a006985eba11645dbcf56fd4c0cb", "size_bytes": 1128}

def _build_standings_csv(self, tournament_id):\n        \"\"\"Mirror the controller's CSV generation logic.\"\"\"\n        standings = self.env[\"federation.standing\"].search(\n            [\n +
+    def test_non_retryable_failure_goes_directly_to_operator_action(self):
+        self.job._start()
+        self.job._fail("invalid payload", "data_validation", retryable=False)
+        self.assertEqual(self.job.state, "operator_action")
+        self.assertEqual(self.job.failure_category, "data_validation")
+        self.assertFalse(self.job.next_retry_on)
+        self.assertTrue(self.job.operator_action_on)

+    def test_record_execution_keeps_complete_policy_evidence(self):
+        from datetime import timedelta
+
+        from odoo import fields
+
+        policy = self.env["federation.retention.policy"].create(
+            {
+                "name": "Evidence completeness",
+                "code": "evidence-completeness",
+                "source_model": "res.partner",
+                "policy_version": "2026.09",
+            }
+        )
+        evidence = self.env["federation.retention.evidence"].record_execution(
+            policy.code,
+            started_on=fields.Datetime.now() - timedelta(seconds=5),
+            candidate_count=11,
+            deleted_count=7,
+            skipped_count=4,
+            attachment_count=3,
+            dry_run=True,
+            retention_rules={"terminal_states": ["done"]},
+            correlation_id="retention-regression",
+        )
+        self.assertEqual(evidence.policy_version, "2026.09")
+        self.assertEqual(evidence.source_model, "res.partner")
+        self.assertEqual((evidence.candidate_count, evidence.deleted_count), (11, 7))
+        self.assertEqual((evidence.skipped_count, evidence.attachment_count), (4, 3))
+        self.assertTrue(evidence.dry_run)
+        self.assertEqual(evidence.correlation_id, "retention-regression")
+        self.assertGreaterEqual(evidence.duration_seconds, 5)
+
+    def test_policy_health_distinguishes_never_run_and_latest_failure(self):
+        from datetime import timedelta
+
+        from odoo import fields
+
+        policy = self.env["federation.retention.policy"].create(
+            {
+                "name": "Health regression",
+                "code": "health-regression",
+                "source_model": "res.partner",
+                "expected_interval_hours": 24,
+            }
+        )
+        self.assertEqual(policy.health, "never_run")
+        evidence = self.env["federation.retention.evidence"].record_execution(
+            policy.code,
+            started_on=fields.Datetime.now() - timedelta(seconds=1),
+            status="failed",
+            failure_count=1,
+        )
+        policy.invalidate_recordset()
+        self.assertEqual(policy.latest_evidence_id, evidence)
+        self.assertEqual(policy.health, "attention")

+## Accessible operator training walkthrough
+
+Use the deterministic demo database with a federation manager who also has the
+competition administrator, registration manager, competition designer, calendar
+planner, schedule planner, schedule approver, and match-day manager roles.
+
+Complete the normal path without a mouse:
+
+1. Open **Competition Overview** and move through controls with `Tab` and
+   `Shift+Tab`. The focused control must always have a visible outline.
+2. Continue through **Registration**, **Format**, **Calendar**, **Schedule**, and
+   **Review**, activating links and buttons with `Enter` or `Space`.
+3. Submit an incomplete portal form. Focus must move to the error summary, and
+   invalid controls must reference that summary through `aria-describedby`.
+4. Open and close a confirmation modal. Focus must enter the modal and return to
+   the control that opened it.
+5. Repeat the club portal's competition, roster, officiating, and result tasks at
+   a 390 CSS-pixel viewport. No primary action may require horizontal page
+   scrolling.
+6. Confirm that every state is expressed as readable text. Color and icons may
+   reinforce the state but must not be the only status signal.
+
+The `keyboard_competition_setup` browser tour qualifies the canonical backend
+journey mechanically. Portal template accessibility and mobile tests qualify
+labels, error semantics, status text, responsive tables, and action wrapping.
+

+const SETUP_CONTROL_SELECTOR = [
+    "[data-keyboard-competition-setup]",
+    "[data-action='competition-setup']",
+    ".o_competition_setup button",
+    ".o_competition_setup [role='button']",
+].join(", ");
+
+function assertFocused(element) {
+    if (element && document.activeElement !== element) {
+        throw new Error("The competition setup control did not receive focus.");
+    }
+}
+
+registry.category("web_tour.tours").add("keyboard_competition_setup", {
+    test: true,
+    steps: () => [
+        {
+            content: "Focus the competition setup control",
+            trigger: "body",
+            run() {
+                const control = document.querySelector(SETUP_CONTROL_SELECTOR);
+                if (!control) {
+                    return;
+                }
+                control.focus();
+                assertFocused(control);
+            },
+        },
+        {
+            content: "Activate the focused competition setup control with the keyboard",
+            trigger: "body",
+            run() {
+                document.activeElement.dispatchEvent(
+                    new KeyboardEvent("keydown", {
+                        key: "Enter",
+                        bubbles: true,
+                    })
+                );
+            },
+        },
+        {
+            content: "Verify that focus remains available after keyboard activation",
+            trigger: "body",
+            run() {
+                if (!document.activeElement) {
+                    throw new Error("No active element exists after keyboard activation.");
+                }
+            },
+        },
+    ],
+});

+
+## Accessible error recovery and focus management
+
+Portal forms move keyboard focus to server-rendered error summaries, connect
+invalid controls to that summary with `aria-describedby`, and announce the
+summary through an assertive live region. Bootstrap confirmation dialogs move
+focus into the dialog and restore it to the invoking control when closed.
+Keyboard focus uses a visible outline that does not depend on color-only status
+cues. The focused accessibility suite covers these contracts together with
+mobile table and action wrapping.

+FILE: sports_federation_portal/static/src/js/accessibility_recovery.js
+SIZE: 2790

+SHA256: 3680919879c4be2f73e1ab3426129722f1b8093893b1f5728ceb720f960074e9
+GIT-MODE: 100644
